# Sajilo Nepali: picture prompts

Every picture in the game, 400 in total. Each prompt below is complete (it already includes the shared style), so you can paste it as is.

## How to make them match

1. **Same tool, same settings** for everything. Square images unless the size column says otherwise.
2. **Attach pandu-namaste as the style reference for everything, and make section 1 (the cast) first.** Then attach the matching cast picture as a reference whenever a prompt mentions that person (Maya, Arjun, Aama, Hajuraama…), so they look the same in every picture.
3. **Round sticker on white:** every game picture sits in a hand-drawn ink circle; I remove the white outside it. Exceptions: cast sheets (plain white), and `app-icon`, `og-image` and sentence scenes (full background).
4. **Keep the file names.** Put the images in `art/` in the repo, or send them to me in batches. I'll shrink them for the web and swap them in.
5. **No words in pictures.** Image tools misspell Nepali, so any Nepali letters (like **अ** on the Letter Master sticker) are added by the app.
6. **Priority if you want to do it in stages:** Pandu → avatars → stickers → word pictures → lesson icons → game icons → sentence scenes (optional).

## Shared style (already inside every prompt)

> A warm, hand-drawn children's storybook illustration with textured dark navy-charcoal ink outlines (#1f2a44) and visible colored-pencil texture with soft watercolor wash fills in warm Himalayan colors: terracotta orange (#e8742a), marigold yellow (#f6b71c), crimson red (#e2453c), royal blue (#2f6fd6), leaf green (#2fa75a) and cream white. People and animals have friendly, gentle faces; objects and food stay simple without faces. Authentic Nepali details. Flat 2D children's book sketch style, no 3D gloss, no gradients, no text, no letters, no numbers, no watermark, cozy and authentic. Circular sticker icon composition: the subject centered inside a loose hand-drawn ink circle on cream paper, with a few tiny doodled leaves or flowers along the circle's edge, isolated on a clean pure white background outside the circle.
